● Update(README.md)
  ⎿  Added 110 lines
       26  - **Cloudflare Pages** — each *user project* gets its own Page
           s project
       27    (and stable `*.pages.dev` URL) on publish, via the Pages API
       28  
       29 +## How It Works                                               
       30 +                                                              
       31 +### Chat → AI generation lifecycle                            
       32 +                                                              
       33 +1. The browser sends `POST /api/projects/:id/chat { message, s
          +tyle }` with                                                  
       34 +   the session cookie (`web/api.js`'s `streamChat`).          
       35 +2. The Worker (`handleChatProject` in `worker/src/index.ts`) l
          +oads the                                                      
       36 +   project's current HTML from its `UserDO`, then builds a pro
          +mpt                                                           
       37 +   (`buildPrompt` in `worker/src/generate.ts`) with three labe
          +led                                                           
       38 +   sections, in this order:                                   
       39 +   - **WEBSITE DEFINITION** — the page's current, complete HTM
          +L.                                                            
       40 +   - **STYLING PREFERENCES** — the plain-English guidance for 
          +the chosen                                                    
       41 +     style preset (`default`/`dark`/`modern`/`minimal`/`playfu
          +l`/                                                           
       42 +     `corporate`; an unrecognized or missing one falls back to
       43 +     `default`).                                              
       44 +   - **REQUESTED CHANGE** — the user's chat message.          
       45 +                                                              
       46 +   The prompt also tells the model to answer in exactly one fo
          +rmat: a                                                       
       47 +   `SUMMARY: <sentence>` line, then the full updated HTML docu
          +ment inside                                                   
       48 +   a ```` ```html ```` fence.                                 
       49 +3. The Worker calls Workers AI — `env.AI.run(MODEL_ID, { promp
          +t, stream:                                                    
       50 +   true, max_tokens })` — via the native `AI` binding declared
          + in                                                           
       51 +   `wrangler.toml`'s `[ai]` block (no separate SDK package; th
          +e binding                                                     
       52 +   is just a method on `env` the Workers runtime provides). Wi
          +th                                                            
       53 +   `stream: true` this resolves to a `ReadableStream` of      
       54 +   Server-Sent-Events bytes (`data: {"response":"..."}\n\n`, e
          +nding                                                         
       55 +   `data: [DONE]\n\n`).                                       
       56 +4. `streamGeneration` decodes that SSE stream and re-emits the
          + raw text                                                     
       57 +   as it arrives, piped straight through as the HTTP response 
          +body —                                                        
       58 +   this is what makes the chat feel live. In parallel, it accu
          +mulates                                                       
       59 +   the full text.                                             
       60 +5. Once the model finishes, a `TransformStream`'s `flush` call
          +back (so                                                      
       61 +   this runs *before* the response stream closes, not after) p
          +ulls the                                                      
       62 +   HTML out of the fence (`extractHtml`) and the one-line summ
          +ary out of                                                    
       63 +   the `SUMMARY:` line (`extractSummary`), then `PUT`s both — 
          +plus the                                                      
       64 +   appended user/assistant `ChatMessage`s — back to the `UserD
          +O`. If                                                        
       65 +   extraction or the save fails, the response stream errors in
          +stead of                                                      
       66 +   closing normally, so the client sees a real failure rather 
          +than a                                                        
       67 +   silent no-op; the project's previous HTML is left untouched
          + either                                                       
       68 +   way.                                                       
       69 +6. The browser only shows the `SUMMARY:` line as the assistant
          +'s chat                                                       
       70 +   message (never the raw HTML) — an animated loading state fi
          +lls in                                                        
       71 +   while the request is in flight. Once it resolves, the front
          +end calls                                                     
       72 +   `GET /api/projects/:id` again and re-renders the (now sandb
          +oxed)                                                         
       73 +   preview iframe with the fresh HTML — the live preview inten
          +tionally                                                      
       74 +   does **not** update token-by-token.                        
       75 +                                                              
       76 +### Publish lifecycle (Cloudflare Pages)                      
       77 +                                                              
       78 +1. The browser sends `POST /api/projects/:id/publish`.        
       79 +2. The Worker runs the stored HTML through a basic abuse filte
          +r                                                             
       80 +   (`checkHtmlForAbuse` — flags, e.g., a form posting to an ex
          +ternal                                                        
       81 +   domain alongside phishing-style language) **before any Clou
          +dflare                                                        
       82 +   API call**; a flagged page is rejected with no Pages projec
          +t touched.                                                    
       83 +3. **First publish only:** the Worker calls the Cloudflare RES
          +T API                                                         
       84 +   directly with `fetch` (not an npm SDK — see below) to creat
          +e a Pages                                                     
       85 +   project: `POST /accounts/:accountId/pages/projects`, `Autho
          +rization:                                                     
       86 +   Bearer <CF_API_TOKEN>`. If the name collides with an existi
          +ng                                                            
       87 +   project, it retries with a numeric suffix (`buildPagesProje
          +ctName` in                                                    
       88 +   `worker/src/slug.ts`, capped under Cloudflare's ~58-charact
          +er project                                                    
       89 +   name limit). The project's stable `*.pages.dev` subdomain (
          +returned                                                      
       90 +   on creation) is saved to the `UserDO` immediately — before 
          +the deploy                                                    
       91 +   step below — so a failed first deploy can't orphan a Pages 
          +project                                                       
       92 +   with nothing in the app pointing at it.                    
       93 +4. **Every publish:** the Worker runs Cloudflare's direct-uplo
          +ad deploy                                                     
       94 +   flow for a single `index.html` (`deployPagesProject` in    
       95 +   `worker/src/cf-pages.ts`):                                 
       96 +   1. `GET /pages/projects/:name/upload-token` → a short-lived
          + upload JWT.                                                  
       97 +   2. `POST /pages/assets/check-missing { hashes }` — which co
          +ntent                                                         
       98 +      hashes does Cloudflare not already have?                
       99 +   3. `POST /pages/assets/upload` — the file content, base64-e
          +ncoded,                                                       
      100 +      only if step 2 said it was missing.                     
      101 +   4. `POST /pages/assets/upsert-hashes` — mark the hash as in
          + use.                                                         
      102 +   5. `POST /pages/projects/:name/deployments` — a multipart r
          +equest                                                        
      103 +      whose `manifest` field maps `/index.html` to that conten
          +t hash,                                                       
      104 +      creating the actual deployment.                         
      105 +5. The Worker stores the project's **stable** subdomain URL (f
          +rom step                                                      
      106 +   3), not the per-deployment URL step 4 returns (which change
          +s on every                                                    
      107 +   publish) — matching the design's "the project keeps its own
      108 +   `*.pages.dev` URL" intent.                                 
      109 +                                                              
      110 +### Delete lifecycle                                          
      111 +                                                              
      112 +`DELETE /api/projects/:id` calls Cloudflare's delete-project e
          +ndpoint                                                       
      113 +only if the project has a Pages project on record (not just "h
          +as been                                                       
      114 +published" — a project that got as far as step 3 above but fai
          +led at                                                        
      115 +step 4 still needs cleanup); a 404 from Cloudflare counts as s
          +uccess                                                        
      116 +(the project is already gone, which is the desired end state).
          + If the                                                       
      117 +delete call fails for any other reason, the Worker returns a r
          +etryable                                                      
      118 +502 and leaves the project in place rather than silently losin
          +g track of                                                    
      119 +a live Pages project.                                         
      120 +                                                              
      121 +### How the Cloudflare "SDK" is used                          
      122 +                                                              
      123 +There's no `cloudflare` npm package anywhere in this project —
          + two                                                          
      124 +different mechanisms are both informally "the SDK":           
      125 +                                                              
      126 +- **Native bindings**, configured in `wrangler.toml` and used 
          +as plain                                                      
      127 +  methods/objects on the Worker's `env`: `env.AI.run(...)` for
          + Workers                                                      
      128 +  AI, `env.USER_DO.idFromName(...)`/`.get(...)` for Durable Ob
          +jects, and                                                    
      129 +  the `[assets]` directory binding that serves `web/`'s static
          + files                                                        
      130 +  from the same Worker. These only exist inside the Workers ru
          +ntime —                                                       
      131 +  there's nothing to `npm install` for them.                  
      132 +- **Plain `fetch` calls to the Cloudflare REST API** for every
          +thing                                                         
      133 +  Pages-related (`worker/src/cf-pages.ts`), authenticated with
          + a Bearer                                                     
      134 +  token (`CF_API_TOKEN`) read from a Worker secret, never sent
          + to the                                                       
      135 +  browser. This is the same HTTP API `wrangler pages deploy` i
          +tself                                                         
      136 +  calls under the hood — this project just calls it directly i
          +nstead of                                                     
      137 +  shelling out to wrangler.                                   
      138 +                                                              
      139  ## Setup
      140  
      141  ```sh

  - Chat → AI generation lifecycle — prompt structure (website definition →
    styling → requested change), the exact Workers AI streaming call and SSE
    format, how the summary and HTML get split apart and persisted before the
    response closes, and why the preview only updates after the full response
    lands.
  - Publish lifecycle — the abuse check gate, first-publish project creation
    with collision retry, the full 5-step Cloudflare Pages direct-upload flow
    (upload-token → check-missing → upload → upsert-hashes → deployments), and
    why the stable subdomain URL (not the per-deployment URL) is what gets
    stored.
  - Delete lifecycle — why it keys off "has a Pages project" rather than "was
    published," and the 404-counts-as-success handling.
  - "How the Cloudflare SDK is used" — clarifying there's no npm SDK: native env
    bindings for AI/Durable Objects/static assets, plus plain fetch calls to
    the Pages REST API for everything publish-related.
